Map cluster to region via channel assignment\n   222→    cluster_regions = chan_beryl[clusters['channels']]\n   223→    return cluster_regions\n   224→\n   225→\n   226→def bin_spikes_vectorized(spike_times, spike_clusters, n_clusters, interval_starts, interval_ends, binsize, n_bins):\n   227→    \"\"\"Bin spikes into trial-aligned time windows. Optimized implementation.\"\"\"\n   228→    n_trials = len(interval_starts)\n   229→    binned = np.zeros((n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n   230→\n   231→    # Pre-sort spikes are already time-sorted after merge\n   232→    for trial_idx in range(n_trials):\n   233→        t_start = interval_starts[trial_idx]\n   234→        t_end = interval_ends[trial_idx]\n   235→\n   236→        if np.isnan(t_start) or np.isnan(t_end):\n   237→            continue\n   238→\n   239→        # Use searchsorted for fast interval selection\n   240→        idx_beg = np.searchsorted(spike_times, t_start, side='left')\n   241→        idx_end = np.searchsorted(spike_times, t_end, side='left')\n   242→\n   243→        if idx_beg >= idx_end:\n   244→            continue\n   245→\n   246→        t_sel = spike_times[idx_beg:idx_end]\n   247→        c_sel = spike_clusters[idx_beg:idx_end]\n   248→\n   249→        # Compute bin indices\n   250→        bin_idx = np.minimum(((t_sel - t_start) / binsize).astype(np.int32), n_bins - 1)\n   251→\n   252→        # Use flat indexing for efficient accumulation\n   253→        valid = (c_sel >= 0) & (c_sel < n_clusters)\n   254→        flat_idx = c_sel[valid] * n_bins + bin_idx[valid]\n   255→        np.add.at(binned[trial_idx].ravel(), flat_idx, 1)\n   256→\n   257→    return binned\n   258→\n   259→\n   260→def interpolate_wheel(timestamps, position, fs=WHEEL_FS, corner_freq=WHEEL_CORNER_FREQ, order=WHEEL_FILTER_ORDER):\n   261→    \"\"\"Interpolate wheel position and compute velocity (matching ibllib).\"\"\"\n   262→    t = np.arange(timestamps[0], timestamps[-1], 1.0 / fs)\n   263→    if len(t) > 0 and t[-1] > timestamps[-1]:\n   264→        t = t[:-1]\n   265→\n   266→    pos_interp = interp1d(timestamps, position, kind='linear')(t)\n   267→\n   268→    # Butterworth low-pass filter for velocity\n   269→    sos = signal.butter(N=order, Wn=corner_freq / fs * 2, btype='lowpass', output='sos')\n   270→    vel = np.insert(np.diff(signal.sosfiltfilt(sos, pos_interp)), 0, 0) * fs\n   271→\n   272→    return t, np.abs(vel).astype(np.float32)  # wheel speed = |velocity|\n   273→\n   274→\n   275→def load_motion_energy(alf_path, side='left'):\n   276→    \"\"\"Load whisker motion energy from camera data.\"\"\"\n   277→    if side == 'left':\n   278→        me_file = find_latest_revision(alf_path, 'leftCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy')\n   279→        times_file = find_latest_revision(alf_path, '_ibl_leftCamera.times.npy')\n   280→    else:\n   281→        me_file = find_latest_revision(alf_path, 'rightCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy')\n   282→        times_file = find_latest_revision(alf_path, '_ibl_rightCamera.times.npy')\n   283→\n   284→    if me_file is None or times_file is None:\n   285→        return None, None\n   286→\n   287→    me = np.load(me_file).flatten()\n   288→    times = np.load(times_file).flatten()\n   289→\n   290→    # Handle length mismatch (common in IBL data)\n   291→    min_len = min(len(me), len(times))\n   292→    return times[:min_len], me[:min_len].astype(np.float32)\n   293→\n   294→\n   295→def interpolate_behavior_to_bins(beh_times, beh_values, interval_starts, interval_ends, binsize, n_bins):\n   296→    \"\"\"Interpolate behavior signal to trial-aligned bins (matching reference code).\"\"\"\n   297→    n_trials = len(interval_starts)\n   298→    result = np.full((n_trials, n_bins), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   299→    good_mask = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=bool)\n   300→\n   301→    for trial_idx in range(n_trials):\n   302→        t_start = interval_starts[trial_idx]\n   303→        t_end = interval_ends[trial_idx]\n   304→\n   305→        if np.isnan(t_start) or np.isnan(t_end):\n   306→            continue\n   307→\n   308→        # Find behavior data in this interval\n   309→        idx_beg = np.searchsorted(beh_times, t_start, side='right')\n   310→        idx_end = np.searchsorted(beh_times, t_end, side='left')\n   311→\n   312→        t_sel = beh_times[idx_beg:idx_end]\n   313→        v_sel = beh_values[idx_beg:idx_end]\n   314→\n   315→        if len(v_sel) == 0:\n   316→            continue\n   317→\n   318→        # Check data availability (matching reference code)\n   319→        if np.abs(t_start - t_sel[0]) > binsize:\n   320→            continue\n   321→        if np.abs(t_end - t_sel[-1]) > binsize:\n   322→            continue\n   323→\n   324→        # Interpolate to uniform bins (matching reference code)\n   325→        x_interp = np.linspace(t_start + binsize, t_end, n_bins)\n   326→        try:\n   327→            y_interp = interp1d(t_sel, v_sel, kind='linear', fill_value='extrapolate')(x_interp)\n   328→            result[trial_idx] = y_interp\n   329→            good_mask[trial_idx] = True\n   330→        except Exception:\n   331→            continue\n   332→\n   333→    return result, good_mask\n   334→\n   335→\n   336→def discretize_to_bins(values, n_bins=N_DISCRETE_BINS):\n   337→    \"\"\"Discretize continuous values into n_bins equal-frequency bins using quantiles.\n   338→    Computed across all valid (non-NaN) values.\"\"\"\n   339→    valid = values[~np.isnan(values)]\n   340→    if len(valid) == 0:\n   341→        return np.zeros_like(values, dtype=int), np.array([])\n   342→\n   343→    # Compute quantile boundaries\n   344→    quantiles = np.linspace(0, 100, n_bins + 1)\n   345→    boundaries = np.percentile(valid, quantiles)\n   346→\n   347→    # Digitize - np.digitize returns values 0..n_bins, clip to 0..n_bins-1\n   348→    result = np.digitize(values, boundaries[1:-1])  # 0 to n_bins-1\n   349→    result = np.clip(result, 0, n_bins - 1)\n   350→\n   351→    return result.astype(int), boundaries\n   352→\n   353→\n   354→def compute_trial_in_block(prob_left):\n   355→    \"\"\"Compute trial number within each block.\n   356→    Block boundaries are where probabilityLeft changes.\"\"\"\n   357→    trial_in_block = np.zeros(len(prob_left), dtype=np.float32)\n   358→    count = 1\n   359→    for i in range(len(prob_left)):\n   360→        if i > 0 and prob_left[i] != prob_left[i-1]:\n   361→            count = 1\n   362→        trial_in_block[i] = count\n   363→        count += 1\n   364→    return trial_in_block\n   365→\n   366→\n   367→def process_session(session_info, br, show_processing=False):\n   368→    \"\"\"Process a single session and return data dict.\"\"\"\n   369→    eid = session_info['eid']\n   370→    subject = session_info['subject']\n   371→    lab = session_info['lab']\n   372→    date = session_info['date']\n   373→\n   374→    t0 = time.time()\n   375→\n   376→    # Find session path\n   377→    alf_path = find_session_path(lab, subject, date)\n   378→    if alf_path is None:\n   379→        print(f\"  Session {eid} ({subject}/{date}): path not found, skipping\")\n   380→        return None\n   381→\n   382→    # Load trials\n   383→    try:\n   384→        trials = load_trials(alf_path)\n   385→    except Exception as e:\n   386→        print(f\"  Session {eid}: error loading trials: {e}\")\n   387→        return None\n   388→\n   389→    # Create trial mask\n   390→    mask = create_trial_mask(trials)\n   391→\n   392→    # Find probes\n   393→    probe_dirs = sorted([d.name for d in alf_path.iterdir() if d.is_dir() and d.name.startswith('probe')])\n   394→    if not probe_dirs:\n   395→        print(f\"  Session {eid}: no probes found, skipping\")\n   396→        return None\n   397→\n   398→    # Load and merge spike sorting from all probes\n   399→    spikes_list = []\n   400→    clusters_list = []\n   401→    for probe_name in probe_dirs:\n   402→        try:\n   403→            spk, clu = load_spike_sorting(alf_path, probe_name)\n   404→            spikes_list.append(spk)\n   405→            clusters_list.append(clu)\n   406→        except Exception as e:\n   407→            print(f\"  Session {eid}, {probe_name}: error loading spikes: {e}\")\n   408→            continue\n   409→\n   410→    if not spikes_list:\n   411→        print(f\"  Session {eid}: no valid probes, skipping\")\n   412→        return None\n   413→\n   414→    spikes, clusters = merge_probes(spikes_list, clusters_list)\n   415→    n_clusters = len(clusters['channels'])\n   416→\n   417→    # Get brain regions\n   418→    cluster_regions = get_brain_regions(clusters, br)\n   419→\n   420→    # Apply trial mask\n   421→    valid_trials = trials[mask].reset_index(drop=True)\n   422→\n   423→    if len(valid_trials) < 2:\n   424→        print(f\"  Session {eid}: fewer than 2 valid trials, skipping\")\n   425→        return None\n   426→\n   427→    # Compute intervals aligned to stimOn_times\n   428→    align_times = valid_trials[ALIGN_TIME].values\n   429→    interval_starts = align_times + TIME_WINDOW[0]\n   430→    interval_ends = align_times + TIME_WINDOW[1]\n   431→\n   432→    # Bin spikes\n   433→    binned_spikes = bin_spikes_vectorized(\n   434→        spikes['times'], spikes['clusters'], n_clusters,\n   435→        interval_starts, interval_ends, BINSIZE, N_BINS\n   436→    )\n   437→    # binned_spikes shape: (n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins)\n   438→\n   439→    # Load wheel data\n   440→    wheel_speed_binned = None\n   441→    try:\n   442→        wheel_pos_raw = np.load(find_latest_revision(alf_path, '_ibl_wheel.position.npy')).flatten()\n   443→        wheel_ts_raw = np.load(find_latest_revision(alf_path, '_ibl_wheel.timestamps.npy')).flatten()\n   444→        wheel_times, wheel_speed = interpolate_wheel(wheel_ts_raw, wheel_pos_raw)\n   445→        wheel_speed_binned, wheel_good = interpolate_behavior_to_bins(\n   446→            wheel_times, wheel_speed, interval_starts, interval_ends, BINSIZE, N_BINS\n   447→        )\n   448→    except Exception as e:\n   449→        print(f\"  Session {eid}: error loading wheel: {e}\")\n   450→\n   451→    # Load whisker motion energy\n   452→    me_binned = None\n   453→    try:\n   454→        me_times, me_values = load_motion_energy(alf_path, side='left')\n   455→        if me_times is None:\n   456→            me_times, me_values = load_motion_energy(alf_path, side='right')\n   457→        if me_times is not None:\n   458→            me_binned, me_good = interpolate_behavior_to_bins(\n   459→                me_times, me_values, interval_starts, interval_ends, BINSIZE, N_BINS\n   460→            )\n   461→    except Exception as e:\n   462→        print(f\"  Session {eid}: error loading motion energy: {e}\")\n   463→\n   464→    # Build combined mask (trials where all data is available)\n   465→    combined_mask = np.ones(len(valid_trials), dtype=bool)\n   466→    if wheel_speed_binned is not None:\n   467→        combined_mask &= ~np.any(np.isnan(wheel_speed_binned), axis=1)\n   468→    else:\n   469→        # If no wheel data, mark all as bad\n   470→        combined_mask[:] = False\n   471→\n   472→    if me_binned is not None:\n   473→        combined_mask &= ~np.any(np.isnan(me_binned), axis=1)\n   474→    else:\n   475→        combined_mask[:] = False\n   476→\n   477→    # Apply combined mask\n   478→    n_valid = combined_mask.sum()\n   479→    if n_valid < 2:\n   480→        print(f\"  Session {eid}: fewer than 2 trials with all data, skipping\")\n   481→        return None\n   482→\n   483→    final_spikes = binned_spikes[combined_mask]  # (n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins)\n   484→    final_wheel = wheel_speed_binned[combined_mask]  # (n_trials, n_bins)\n   485→    final_me = me_binned[combined_mask]  # (n_trials, n_bins)\n   486→    final_trials = valid_trials[combined_mask].reset_index(drop=True)\n   487→\n   488→    # Build neural data: list of (n_neurons, n_timepoints) per trial\n   489→    # Use uint8 to save memory (spike counts per 20ms bin are small integers, max ~12)\n   490→    neural_trials = []\n   491→    for t in range(len(final_spikes)):\n   492→        neural_trials.append(final_spikes[t].astype(np.uint8))  # (n_clusters, n_bins)\n   493→\n   494→    # Build inputs\n   495→    # Input 0: time since stimulus onset (continuous, time-varying)\n   496→    time_since_stim = np.linspace(TIME_WINDOW[0] + BINSIZE, TIME_WINDOW[1], N_BINS).astype(np.float32)\n   497→\n   498→    # Input 1: trial number in block (continuous, per-trial)\n   499→    prob_left_all = trials['probabilityLeft'].values\n   500→    trial_in_block_all = compute_trial_in_block(prob_left_all)\n   501→    # Apply masks to get trial_in_block for valid+combined trials\n   502→    trial_in_block_valid = trial_in_block_all[mask.values]\n   503→    trial_in_block_final = trial_in_block_valid[combined_mask]\n   504→\n   505→    input_trials = []\n   506→    for t in range(len(final_trials)):\n   507→        inp = np.array([\n   508→            time_since_stim,  # time-varying\n   509→            np.full(N_BINS, trial_in_block_final[t], dtype=np.float32),  # per-trial, broadcast to time\n   510→        ], dtype=np.float32)  # (2, n_bins)\n   511→        input_trials.append(inp)\n   512→\n   513→    # Build outputs\n   514→    # Output 0: choice (binary, per-trial) - left=0, right=1\n   515→    choice = final_trials['choice'].values.copy()\n   516→    # IBL: -1=left, 1=right -> convert to 0=left, 1=right\n   517→    choice_encoded = ((choice + 1) // 2).astype(int)  # -1->0, 1->1\n   518→\n   519→    # Output 1: prior probability of left (per-trial, categorical)\n   520→    prob_left = final_trials['probabilityLeft'].values\n   521→    prior_encoded = np.zeros(len(prob_left), dtype=int)\n   522→    prior_encoded[prob_left == 0.2] = 0\n   523→    prior_encoded[prob_left == 0.5] = 1\n   524→    prior_encoded[prob_left == 0.8] = 2\n   525→\n   526→    # Output 2: wheel speed discretized into 3 bins (time-varying)\n   527→    # Discretize using quantiles across ALL timepoints in this session\n   528→    all_wheel_flat = final_wheel.flatten()\n   529→    wheel_disc, wheel_boundaries = discretize_to_bins(all_wheel_flat, N_DISCRETE_BINS)\n   530→    wheel_disc_2d = wheel_disc.reshape(final_wheel.shape).astype(int)\n   531→\n   532→    # Output 3: whisker motion energy discretized into 3 bins (time-varying)\n   533→    all_me_flat = final_me.flatten()\n   534→    me_disc, me_boundaries = discretize_to_bins(all_me_flat, N_DISCRETE_BINS)\n   535→    me_disc_2d = me_disc.reshape(final_me.shape).astype(int)\n   536→\n   537→    output_trials = []\n   538→    for t in range(len(final_trials)):\n   539→        out = np.array([\n   540→            np.full(N_BINS, choice_encoded[t], dtype=int),  # per-trial, broadcast\n   541→            np.full(N_BINS, prior_encoded[t], dtype=int),  # per-trial, broadcast\n   542→            wheel_disc_2d[t],  # time-varying\n   543→            me_disc_2d[t],  # time-varying\n   544→        ], dtype=int)  # (4, n_bins)\n   545→        output_trials.append(out)\n   546→\n   547→    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n   548→    print(f\"  Session {eid} ({subject}/{date}): {n_valid} trials, {n_clusters} neurons, {elapsed:.1f}s\")\n   549→\n   550→    # Processing visualization\n   551→    if show_processing:\n   552→        try:\n   553→            plot_processing(eid, subject, date, final_spikes, final_wheel, final_me,\n   554→                          wheel_disc_2d, me_disc_2d, choice_encoded, prior_encoded,\n   555→                          time_since_stim, cluster_regions)\n   556→        except Exception as e:\n   557→            print(f\"  Warning: could not create processing plots: {e}\")\n   558→\n   559→    return {\n   560→        'neural': neural_trials,\n   561→        'input': input_trials,\n   562→        'output': output_trials,\n   563→        'subject': subject,\n   564→        'cluster_regions': cluster_regions,\n   565→        'n_clusters': n_clusters,\n   566→        'n_trials': n_valid,\n   567→        'eid': eid,\n   568→    }\n   569→\n   570→\n   571→def plot_processing(eid, subject, date, spikes, wheel, me, wheel_disc, me_disc,\n   572→                   choice, prior, time_axis, cluster_regions):\n   573→    \"\"\"Plot processing steps for visual verification.\"\"\"\n   574→    import matplotlib\n   575→    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   576→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   577→\n   578→    fig, axes = plt.subplots(4, 3, figsize=(18, 16))\n   579→    fig.suptitle(f'Processing: {subject}/{date} ({eid[:8]}...)', fontsize=14)\n   580→\n   581→    # Trial 0 and 1\n   582→    for col, trial_idx in enumerate([0, min(1, len(spikes)-1)]):\n   583→        # Neural activity (first 50 neurons)\n   584→        n_show = min(50, spikes.shape[1])\n   585→        axes[0, col].imshow(spikes[trial_idx, :n_show, :], aspect='auto', cmap='hot',\n   586→                           extent=[time_axis[0], time_axis[-1], n_show, 0])\n   587→        axes[0, col].set_title(f'Trial {trial_idx}: Neural ({n_show}/{spikes.shape[1]} neurons)')\n   588→        axes[0, col].set_xlabel('Time from stimOn (s)')\n   589→\n   590→        # Wheel speed (raw vs discretized)\n   591→        axes[1, col].plot(time_axis, wheel[trial_idx], 'b-', alpha=0.5, label='Raw wheel speed')\n   592→        axes[1, col].plot(time_axis, wheel_disc[trial_idx], 'r-', label='Discretized')\n   593→        axes[1, col].set_title(f'Trial {trial_idx}: Wheel speed')\n   594→        axes[1, col].legend(fontsize=8)\n   595→\n   596→        # Whisker ME (raw vs discretized)\n   597→        axes[2, col].plot(time_axis, me[trial_idx], 'b-', alpha=0.5, label='Raw ME')\n   598→        axes[2, col].plot(time_axis, me_disc[trial_idx], 'r-', label='Discretized')\n   599→        axes[2, col].set_title(f'Trial {trial_idx}: Whisker ME')\n   600→        axes[2, col].legend(fontsize=8)\n   601→\n   602→    # Summary statistics\n   603→    # Choice distribution\n   604→    axes[0, 2].bar(['Left (0)', 'Right (1)'],\n   605→                   [np.sum(choice == 0), np.sum(choice == 1)])\n   606→    axes[0, 2].set_title('Choice distribution')\n   607→\n   608→    # Prior distribution\n   609→    axes[1, 2].bar(['0.2 (0)', '0.5 (1)', '0.8 (2)'],\n   610→                   [np.sum(prior == 0), np.sum(prior == 1), np.sum(prior == 2)])\n   611→    axes[1, 2].set_title('Prior distribution')\n   612→\n   613→    # Wheel speed histogram\n   614→    axes[2, 2].hist(wheel.flatten(), bins=50, alpha=0.7)\n   615→    axes[2, 2].set_title('Wheel speed distribution')\n   616→\n   617→    # Brain regions\n   618→    unique_regions, counts = np.unique(cluster_regions, return_counts=True)\n   619→    sort_idx = np.argsort(-counts)[:15]  # Top 15\n   620→    axes[3, 0].barh(range(len(sort_idx)), counts[sort_idx])\n   621→    axes[3, 0].set_yticks(range(len(sort_idx)))\n   622→    axes[3, 0].set_yticklabels(unique_regions[sort_idx], fontsize=7)\n   623→    axes[3, 0].set_title('Top brain regions')\n   624→\n   625→    # ME distribution\n   626→    axes[3, 1].hist(me.flatten(), bins=50, alpha=0.7)\n   627→    axes[3, 1].set_title('Whisker ME distribution')\n   628→\n   629→    # Mean neural activity across trials\n   630→    axes[3, 2].plot(time_axis, spikes.mean(axis=(0, 1)), 'k-')\n   631→    axes[3, 2].set_title('Mean firing rate')\n   632→    axes[3, 2].set_xlabel('Time from stimOn (s)')\n   633→\n   634→    fig.tight_layout()\n   635→    safe_eid = eid[:8]\n   636→    fig.savefig(f'/app/processing_{safe_eid}.png', dpi=150)\n   637→    plt.close(fig)\n   638→    print(f\"  Saved processing plot: processing_{safe_eid}.png\")\n   639→\n   640→\n   641→def get_session_list(bwm_df):\n   642→    \"\"\"Get unique sessions from BWM release CSV.\"\"\"\n   643→    # Group by eid to get one row per session\n   644→    sessions = bwm_df.groupby('eid').first().reset_index()\n   645→    return sessions\n   646→\n   647→\n   648→def main():\n   649→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n   650→    parser.add_argument('output', type=str, help='Output pickle file path')\n   651→    parser.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True, help='Process all sessions (default)')\n   652→    parser.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='Process only 2 sessions for testing')\n   653→    parser.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true', help='Plot processing visualizations')\n   654→    args = parser.parse_args()\n   655→\n   656→    if args.sample:\n   657→        args.full = False\n   658→\n   659→    print(\"=\" * 60)\n   660→    print(\"IBL Brain-Wide Map Data Conversion\")\n   661→    print(\"=\" * 60)\n   662→\n   663→    t_start = time.time()\n   664→\n   665→    # Load BWM release info\n   666→    bwm_df = pd.read_csv(BWM_CSV, index_col=0)\n   667→    sessions = get_session_list(bwm_df)\n   668→    print(f\"Total sessions in BWM release: {len(sessions)}\")\n   669→\n   670→    if args.sample:\n   671→        # Pick 2 sessions for testing\n   672→        np.random.seed(42)\n   673→        sample_idx = np.random.choice(len(sessions), min(2, len(sessions)), replace=False)\n   674→        sessions = sessions.iloc[sample_idx].reset_index(drop=True)\n   675→        print(f\"Sample mode: processing {len(sessions)} sessions\")\n   676→\n   677→    # Initialize brain atlas\n   678→    br = BrainRegions()\n   679→\n   680→    # Process sessions\n   681→    all_results = []\n   682→    for idx in range(len(sessions)):\n   683→        sess = sessions.iloc[idx]\n   684→        print(f\"\\nProcessing session {idx+1}/{len(sessions)}: {sess['subject']}/{sess['date']}\")\n   685→        result = process_session(sess, br, show_processing=args.show_processing)\n   686→        if result is not None:\n   687→            all_results.append(result)\n   688→\n   689→        if (idx + 1) % 10 == 0:\n   690→            elapsed = time.time() - t_start\n   691→            rate = elapsed / (idx + 1)\n   692→            remaining = rate * (len(sessions) - idx - 1)\n   693→            print(f\"\\n  Progress: {idx+1}/{len(sessions)} sessions, \"\n   694→                  f\"{elapsed:.0f}s elapsed, ~{remaining:.0f}s remaining\")\n   695→\n   696→    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n   697→    print(f\"Successfully processed {len(all_results)} / {len(sessions)} sessions\")\n   698→\n   699→    if len(all_results) == 0:\n   700→        print(\"ERROR: No sessions processed successfully!\")\n   701→        sys.exit(1)\n   702→\n   703→    # Build final data structure\n   704→    print(\"\\nBuilding final data structure...\")\n   705→\n   706→    # Collect all unique subjects\n   707→    all_subjects = sorted(set(r['subject'] for r in all_results))\n   708→    subject_to_idx = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(all_subjects)}\n   709→\n   710→    # Collect all unique brain regions\n   711→    all_regions_set = set()\n   712→    for r in all_results:\n   713→        all_regions_set.update(r['cluster_regions'])\n   714→    all_regions = sorted(all_regions_set)\n   715→    region_to_idx = {r: i for i, r in enumerate(all_regions)}\n   716→\n   717→    # Build lists\n   718→    neural_list = []\n   719→    input_list = []\n   720→    output_list = []\n   721→    subject_idx = []\n   722→    brain_region_idx = []\n   723→\n   724→    for r in all_results:\n   725→        neural_list.append(r['neural'])\n   726→        input_list.append(r['input'])\n   727→        output_list.append(r['output'])\n   728→        subject_idx.append(subject_to_idx[r['subject']])\n   729→        brain_region_idx.append(np.array([region_to_idx[reg] for reg in r['cluster_regions']]))\n   730→\n   731→    data = {\n   732→        'neural': neural_list,\n   733→        'input': input_list,\n   734→        'output': output_list,\n   735→        'subjects': all_subjects,\n   736→        'subject_idx': np.array(subject_idx),\n   737→        'brain_regions': all_regions,\n   738→        'brain_region_idx': brain_region_idx,\n   739→        'input_names': ['time_since_stimulus_onset', 'trial_number_in_block'],\n   740→        'output_names': ['choice', 'prior_probability_left', 'wheel_speed', 'whisker_motion_energy'],\n   741→        'output_values': [\n   742→            ['left', 'right'],                  # choice: 0=left, 1=right\n   743→            ['0.2', '0.5', '0.8'],              # prior: 0=0.2, 1=0.5, 2=0.8\n   744→            ['low', 'medium', 'high'],           # wheel speed: 3 quantile bins\n   745→            ['low', 'medium', 'high'],           # whisker ME: 3 quantile bins\n   746→        ],\n   747→        'metadata': {\n   748→            'task_description': 'IBL decision-making task: mice rotate wheel to indicate location of visual stimulus',\n   749→            'time_bin_size': BINSIZE * 1000,  # in ms\n   750→            'temporal_alignment_event': 'stimulus onset (stimOn_times)',\n   751→            'off_start': TIME_WINDOW[0],\n   752→            'off_end': TIME_WINDOW[1],\n   753→            'n_timepoints': N_BINS,\n   754→            'dataset': 'IBL Brain-Wide Map',\n   755→            'n_sessions': len(all_results),\n   756→            'n_subjects': len(all_subjects),\n   757→            'total_trials': sum(r['n_trials'] for r in all_results),\n   758→            'total_neurons': sum(r['n_clusters'] for r in all_results),\n   759→        }\n   760→    }\n   761→\n   762→    # Print summary\n   763→    print(f\"\\nDataset Summary:\")\n   764→    print(f\"  Sessions: {len(all_results)}\")\n   765→    print(f\"  Subjects: {len(all_subjects)}\")\n   766→    print(f\"  Total trials: {data['metadata']['total_trials']}\")\n   767→    print(f\"  Total neurons: {data['metadata']['total_neurons']}\")\n   768→    print(f\"  Brain regions: {len(all_regions)}\")\n   769→    print(f\"  Time bins: {N_BINS} ({BINSIZE*1000:.0f} ms each)\")\n   770→    print(f\"  Inputs: {data['input_names']}\")\n   771→    print(f\"  Outputs: {data['output_names']}\")\n   772→\n   773→    # Check revision directories (format: #YYYY-MM-DD#)\n    candidates = []\n    if base_path.exists():\n        for item in base_path.iterdir():\n            if item.is_dir() and item.name.startswith('#') and item.name.endswith('#'):\n                for f in item.iterdir():\n                    if filename_pattern in f.name:\n                        candidates.append(f)\n        # Also check base path directly\n        for f in base_path.iterdir():\n            if not f.is_dir() and filename_pattern in f.name:\n                candidates.append(f)\n\n    if not candidates:\n        return None\n    # Sort by revision date (newest first), files without revision come last\n    def sort_key(p):\n        parts = p.parts\n        for part in parts:\n            if part.startswith('#') and part.endswith('#'):\n                return part\n        return '#0000-00-00#'\n    candidates.sort(key=sort_key, reverse=True)\n    return candidates[0]\n\n\ndef load_trials(alf_path):\n    \"\"\"Load trials table from session.\"\"\"\n    trials_file = find_latest_revision(alf_path, '_ibl_trials.table.pqt')\n    if trials_file is None:\n        raise FileNotFoundError(f\"No trials table found in {alf_path}\")\n    trials = pd.read_parquet(trials_file)\n    return trials\n\n\ndef create_trial_mask(trials):\n    \"\"\"Create boolean mask for trial inclusion following reference code.\"\"\"\n    mask = pd.Series(True, index=trials.index)\n\n    # Exclude NaN in key events\n    for event in NAN_EXCLUDE:\n        if event in trials.columns:\n            mask &= ~trials[event].isna()\n\n    # Reaction time filter\n    rt = trials['firstMovement_times'] - trials['stimOn_times']\n    mask &= (rt >= MIN_RT) & (rt <= MAX_RT)\n\n    # Map cluster to region via channel assignment\n    cluster_regions = chan_beryl[clusters['channels']]\n    return cluster_regions\n\n\ndef bin_spikes_vectorized(spike_times, spike_clusters, n_clusters, interval_starts, interval_ends, binsize, n_bins):\n    \"\"\"Bin spikes into trial-aligned time windows. Optimized implementation.\"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(interval_starts)\n    binned = np.zeros((n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n\n    # Pre-sort spikes are already time-sorted after merge\n    for trial_idx in range(n_trials):\n        t_start = interval_starts[trial_idx]\n        t_end = interval_ends[trial_idx]\n\n        if np.isnan(t_start) or np.isnan(t_end):\n            continue\n\n        # Use searchsorted for fast interval selection\n        idx_beg = np.searchsorted(spike_times, t_start, side='left')\n        idx_end = np.searchsorted(spike_times, t_end, side='left')\n\n        if idx_beg >= idx_end:\n            continue\n\n        t_sel = spike_times[idx_beg:idx_end]\n        c_sel = spike_clusters[idx_beg:idx_end]\n\n        # Compute bin indices\n        bin_idx = np.minimum(((t_sel - t_start) / binsize).astype(np.int32), n_bins - 1)\n\n        # Use flat indexing for efficient accumulation\n        valid = (c_sel >= 0) & (c_sel < n_clusters)\n        flat_idx = c_sel[valid] * n_bins + bin_idx[valid]\n        np.add.at(binned[trial_idx].ravel(), flat_idx, 1)\n\n    return binned\n\n\ndef interpolate_wheel(timestamps, position, fs=WHEEL_FS, corner_freq=WHEEL_CORNER_FREQ, order=WHEEL_FILTER_ORDER):\n    \"\"\"Interpolate wheel position and compute velocity (matching ibllib).\"\"\"\n    t = np.arange(timestamps[0], timestamps[-1], 1.0 / fs)\n    if len(t) > 0 and t[-1] > timestamps[-1]:\n        t = t[:-1]\n\n    pos_interp = interp1d(timestamps, position, kind='linear')(t)\n\n    # Butterworth low-pass filter for velocity\n    sos = signal.butter(N=order, Wn=corner_freq / fs * 2, btype='lowpass', output='sos')\n    vel = np.insert(np.diff(signal.sosfiltfilt(sos, pos_interp)), 0, 0) * fs\n\n    return t, np.abs(vel).astype(np.float32)  # wheel speed = |velocity|\n\n\ndef load_motion_energy(alf_path, side='left'):\n    \"\"\"Load whisker motion energy from camera data.\"\"\"\n    if side == 'left':\n        me_file = find_latest_revision(alf_path, 'leftCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy')\n        times_file = find_latest_revision(alf_path, '_ibl_leftCamera.times.npy')\n    else:\n        me_file = find_latest_revision(alf_path, 'rightCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy')\n        times_file = find_latest_revision(alf_path, '_ibl_rightCamera.times.npy')\n\n    if me_file is None or times_file is None:\n        return None, None\n\n    me = np.load(me_file).flatten()\n    times = np.load(times_file).flatten()\n\n    # Handle length mismatch (common in IBL data)\n    min_len = min(len(me), len(times))\n    return times[:min_len], me[:min_len].astype(np.float32)\n\n\ndef interpolate_behavior_to_bins(beh_times, beh_values, interval_starts, interval_ends, binsize, n_bins):\n    \"\"\"Interpolate behavior signal to trial-aligned bins (matching reference code).\"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(interval_starts)\n    result = np.full((n_trials, n_bins), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n    good_mask = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=bool)\n\n    for trial_idx in range(n_trials):\n        t_start = interval_starts[trial_idx]\n        t_end = interval_ends[trial_idx]\n\n        if np.isnan(t_start) or np.isnan(t_end):\n            continue\n\n        # Find behavior data in this interval\n        idx_beg = np.searchsorted(beh_times, t_start, side='right')\n        idx_end = np.searchsorted(beh_times, t_end, side='left')\n\n        t_sel = beh_times[idx_beg:idx_end]\n        v_sel = beh_values[idx_beg:idx_end]\n\n        if len(v_sel) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Check data availability (matching reference code)\n        if np.abs(t_start - t_sel[0]) > binsize:\n            continue\n        if np.abs(t_end - t_sel[-1]) > binsize:\n            continue\n\n        # Interpolate to uniform bins (matching reference code)\n        x_interp = np.linspace(t_start + binsize, t_end, n_bins)\n        try:\n            y_interp = interp1d(t_sel, v_sel, kind='linear', fill_value='extrapolate')(x_interp)\n            result[trial_idx] = y_interp\n            good_mask[trial_idx] = True\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n\n    return result, good_mask\n\n\ndef discretize_to_bins(values, n_bins=N_DISCRETE_BINS):\n    \"\"\"Discretize continuous values into n_bins equal-frequency bins using quantiles.\n    Computed across all valid (non-NaN) values.\"\"\"\n    valid = values[~np.isnan(values)]\n    if len(valid) == 0:\n        return np.zeros_like(values, dtype=int), np.array([])\n\n    # Compute quantile boundaries\n    quantiles = np.linspace(0, 100, n_bins + 1)\n    boundaries = np.percentile(valid, quantiles)\n\n    # Digitize - np.digitize returns values 0..n_bins, clip to 0..n_bins-1\n    result = np.digitize(values, boundaries[1:-1])  # 0 to n_bins-1\n    result = np.clip(result, 0, n_bins - 1)\n\n    return result.astype(int), boundaries\n\n\ndef compute_trial_in_block(prob_left):\n    \"\"\"Compute trial number within each block.\n    Block boundaries are where probabilityLeft changes.\"\"\"\n    trial_in_block = np.zeros(len(prob_left), dtype=np.float32)\n    count = 1\n    for i in range(len(prob_left)):\n        if i > 0 and prob_left[i] != prob_left[i-1]:\n            count = 1\n        trial_in_block[i] = count\n        count += 1\n    return trial_in_block\n\n\ndef process_session(session_info, br, show_processing=False):\n    \"\"\"Process a single session and return data dict.\"\"\"\n    eid = session_info['eid']\n    subject = session_info['subject']\n    lab = session_info['lab']\n    date = session_info['date']\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    # Find session path\n    alf_path = find_session_path(lab, subject, date)\n    if alf_path is None:\n        print(f\"  Session {eid} ({subject}/{date}): path not found, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # Load trials\n    try:\n        trials = load_trials(alf_path)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  Session {eid}: error loading trials: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Create trial mask\n    mask = create_trial_mask(trials)\n\n    # Load and merge spike sorting from all probes\n    spikes_list = []\n    clusters_list = []\n    for probe_name in probe_dirs:\n        try:\n            spk, clu = load_spike_sorting(alf_path, probe_name)\n            spikes_list.append(spk)\n            clusters_list.append(clu)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  Session {eid}, {probe_name}: error loading spikes: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    if not spikes_list:\n        print(f\"  Session {eid}: no valid probes, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    spikes, clusters = merge_probes(spikes_list, clusters_list)\n    n_clusters = len(clusters['channels'])\n\n    # Get brain regions\n    cluster_regions = get_brain_regions(clusters, br)\n\n    # Apply trial mask\n    valid_trials = trials[mask].reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    if len(valid_trials) < 2:\n        print(f\"  Session {eid}: fewer than 2 valid trials, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # Compute intervals aligned to stimOn_times\n    align_times = valid_trials[ALIGN_TIME].values\n    interval_starts = align_times + TIME_WINDOW[0]\n    interval_ends = align_times + TIME_WINDOW[1]\n\n    # Bin spikes\n    binned_spikes = bin_spikes_vectorized(\n        spikes['times'], spikes['clusters'], n_clusters,\n        interval_starts, interval_ends, BINSIZE, N_BINS\n    )\n    # binned_spikes shape: (n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins)\n\n    # Load wheel data\n    wheel_speed_binned = None\n    try:\n        wheel_pos_raw = np.load(find_latest_revision(alf_path, '_ibl_wheel.position.npy')).flatten()\n        wheel_ts_raw = np.load(find_latest_revision(alf_path, '_ibl_wheel.timestamps.npy')).flatten()\n        wheel_times, wheel_speed = interpolate_wheel(wheel_ts_raw, wheel_pos_raw)\n        wheel_speed_binned, wheel_good = interpolate_behavior_to_bins(\n            wheel_times, wheel_speed, interval_starts, interval_ends, BINSIZE, N_BINS\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  Session {eid}: error loading wheel: {e}\")\n\n    # Load whisker motion energy\n    me_binned = None\n    try:\n        me_times, me_values = load_motion_energy(alf_path, side='left')\n        if me_times is None:\n            me_times, me_values = load_motion_energy(alf_path, side='right')\n        if me_times is not None:\n            me_binned, me_good = interpolate_behavior_to_bins(\n                me_times, me_values, interval_starts, interval_ends, BINSIZE, N_BINS\n            )\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  Session {eid}: error loading motion energy: {e}\")\n\n    # Build combined mask (trials where all data is available)\n    combined_mask = np.ones(len(valid_trials), dtype=bool)\n    if wheel_speed_binned is not None:\n        combined_mask &= ~np.any(np.isnan(wheel_speed_binned), axis=1)\n    else:\n        # If no wheel data, mark all as bad\n        combined_mask[:] = False\n\n    if me_binned is not None:\n        combined_mask &= ~np.any(np.isnan(me_binned), axis=1)\n    else:\n        combined_mask[:] = False\n\n    # Apply combined mask\n    n_valid = combined_mask.sum()\n    if n_valid < 2:\n        print(f\"  Session {eid}: fewer than 2 trials with all data, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    final_spikes = binned_spikes[combined_mask]  # (n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins)\n    final_wheel = wheel_speed_binned[combined_mask]  # (n_trials, n_bins)\n    final_me = me_binned[combined_mask]  # (n_trials, n_bins)\n    final_trials = valid_trials[combined_mask].reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    # Build neural data: list of (n_neurons, n_timepoints) per trial\n    # Use uint8 to save memory (spike counts per 20ms bin are small integers, max ~12)\n    neural_trials = []\n    for t in range(len(final_spikes)):\n        neural_trials.append(final_spikes[t].astype(np.uint8))  # (n_clusters, n_bins)\n\n    # Build inputs\n    # Input 0: time since stimulus onset (continuous, time-varying)\n    time_since_stim = np.linspace(TIME_WINDOW[0] + BINSIZE, TIME_WINDOW[1], N_BINS).astype(np.float32)\n\n    # Input 1: trial number in block (continuous, per-trial)\n    prob_left_all = trials['probabilityLeft'].values\n    trial_in_block_all = compute_trial_in_block(prob_left_all)\n    # Apply masks to get trial_in_block for valid+combined trials\n    trial_in_block_valid = trial_in_block_all[mask.values]\n    trial_in_block_final = trial_in_block_valid[combined_mask]\n\n    input_trials = []\n    for t in range(len(final_trials)):\n        inp = np.array([\n            time_since_stim,  # time-varying\n            np.full(N_BINS, trial_in_block_final[t], dtype=np.float32),  # per-trial, broadcast to time\n        ], dtype=np.float32)  # (2, n_bins)\n        input_trials.append(inp)\n\n    # Build outputs\n    # Output 0: choice (binary, per-trial) - left=0, right=1\n    choice = final_trials['choice'].values.copy()\n    # IBL: -1=left, 1=right -> convert to 0=left, 1=right\n    choice_encoded = ((choice + 1) // 2).astype(int)  # -1->0, 1->1\n\n    # Output 1: prior probability of left (per-trial, categorical)\n    prob_left = final_trials['probabilityLeft'].values\n    prior_encoded = np.zeros(len(prob_left), dtype=int)\n    prior_encoded[prob_left == 0.2] = 0\n    prior_encoded[prob_left == 0.5] = 1\n    prior_encoded[prob_left == 0.8] = 2\n\n    # Output 2: wheel speed discretized into 3 bins (time-varying)\n    # Discretize using quantiles across ALL timepoints in this session\n    all_wheel_flat = final_wheel.flatten()\n    wheel_disc, wheel_boundaries = discretize_to_bins(all_wheel_flat, N_DISCRETE_BINS)\n    wheel_disc_2d = wheel_disc.reshape(final_wheel.shape).astype(int)\n\n    # decoding window around stimulus onset, following the reference code\nBIN = 0.02\nN_BINS = int(round((T_STOP - T_START) / BIN))            # 100\nEDGES = T_START + BIN * np.arange(N_BINS + 1)\nTIME = EDGES[:-1] + BIN / 2                              # equal sized classes, from percentiles of each session's own trace\n\nCHOICE = {1.0: 0, -1.0: 1}       # +1 is a leftward choice, -1 rightward; 0 is a no response, dropped\nPRIOR = {0.2: 0, 0.5: 1, 0.8: 2}\nMOVEMENT = ['low', 'medium', 'high']\n\nINPUT_NAMES = ['time_from_stimulus_onset', 'trial_number_in_block']\nOUTPUT_NAMES = ['choice', 'prior_probability_left', 'wheel_speed', 'whisker_motion_energy']\nOUTPUT_VALUES = [['left', 'right'], ['0.2', '0.5', '0.8'], MOVEMENT, MOVEMENT]\n\n# the cache location is not known until --datadir is parsed, and the connection cannot be\n# shared across a fork anyway, so connect() fills this in per process rather than at import\none = None\nbrain_regions = BrainRegions()\n\n\ndef connect(datadir):\n    \"\"\"Point the module-level ONE client at the cache under datadir.\n\n    datadir: directory holding the one_cache/ folder.\n    Sets the global `one`; returns nothing.\n    \"\"\"\n    global one\n    one = ONE(base_url='https://openalyx.internationalbrainlab.org', password='international',\n              silent=True, cache_dir=os.path.join(datadir, 'one_cache'))\n\n\n# --- sessions ---\n\ndef select_sessions():\n    \"\"\"Released sessions that have everything the decoder needs, grouped by subject.\"\"\"\n    one.load_cache(tag=RELEASE)               # probabilityLeft is constant within a block, so a change of value starts a new block\n    block = (trials.probabilityLeft != trials.probabilityLeft.shift()).cumsum()\n\n    return pd.DataFrame({'choice': trials.choice.map(CHOICE),\n                         'prior': trials.probabilityLeft.map(PRIOR),\n                         'trial_in_block': trials.groupby(block).cumcount()})\n\n\ndef covered(times, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Trials whose window is spanned by this stream, allowing the one bin gap the reference allows.\"\"\"\n    start, stop = stim_on + T_START, stim_on + T_STOP\n    begin = np.searchsorted(times, start, side='right')      # first sample inside the window\n    end = np.searchsorted(times, stop, side='left') - 1       # last sample inside the window\n\n    # a trial with no sample at all, or a gap at either edge, has no usable trace\n    inside = begin <= end\n    first = np.where(inside, times[np.clip(begin, 0, times.size - 1)], np.inf)\n    last = np.where(inside, times[np.clip(end, 0, times.size - 1)], -np.inf)\n    return inside & (np.abs(start - first) <= BIN) & (np.abs(stop - last) <= BIN)\n\n\ndef usable_trials(trials, wheel_times, camera_times):\n    \"\"\"Trials the paper would keep, that also have a full wheel and camera window.\"\"\"\n    stim_on = trials.stimOn_times.to_numpy()\n    reaction = trials.firstMovement_times.to_numpy() - stim_on\n\n    keep = (reaction >= MIN_RT) & (reaction <= MAX_RT)          # NaN compares false, so those go too\n    keep &= trials.choice.isin(CHOICE).to_numpy()               # drops the no response trials\n    keep &= trials.probabilityLeft.isin(PRIOR).to_numpy()\n\n    return keep & covered(wheel_times, stim_on) & covered(camera_times, stim_on)\n\n\n# --- behavior ---\n\ndef window_slice(times, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Index range of each trial window, so a stream is sliced rather than rescanned per trial.\"\"\"\n    window_start = stim_on + T_START\n    window_stop = stim_on + T_STOP\n\n    # the samples are in time order, so each window is one contiguous range of them\n    begin = np.searchsorted(times, window_start)\n    end = np.searchsorted(times, window_stop)\n\n    return begin, end\n\n\ndef trial_traces(times, values, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"A continuous trace resampled onto the same time grid for every trial.\"\"\"\n    begin, end = window_slice(times, stim_on)\n\n    traces = []\n    for trial, onset in enumerate(stim_on):\n        # one sample either side of the window, so the first and last bin centre are\n        # interpolated rather than clamped to the nearest sample inside it\n        first = max(begin[trial] - 1, 0)\n        last = end[trial] + 1\n\n        traces.append(np.interp(onset + TIME, times[first:last], values[first:last]))\n\n    return np.array(traces)\n\n\ndef discretize(trace):\n    \"\"\"Equal sized classes, split at percentiles of this session's own trace.\"\"\"\n    return np.digitize(trace, np.percentile(trace, SPLIT))\n\n\n# --- neural ---\n\ndef load_neural(eid):\n    \"\"\"Spike times and good clusters of a session, merged over its probes.\"\"\"\n    times, units, regions = [], [], []\n    for pid, name in zip(*one.eid2pid(eid)):\n        loader = SpikeSortingLoader(pid=pid, one=one, eid=eid, pname=name)\n\n        # skip the md5 check, which rereads every spike file and draws its own progress bar\n        spikes, clusters, channels = loader.load_spike_sorting(check_hash=False)\n\n        # a session can hold an insertion whose spike sorting was never released\n        if not clusters:\n            continue\n\n        clusters = loader.merge_clusters(spikes, clusters, channels)   # renumber the surviving clusters, continuing the count from the previous probe\n        new_index = np.cumsum(is_good) - 1\n        units.append(new_index[spikes['clusters'][keep]] + len(regions))\n        times.append(spikes['times'][keep])\n\n        # Beryl is the coarser atlas the reference code uses, e.g. DG-sg and DG-mo both become DG\n        regions.extend(brain_regions.acronym2acronym(clusters['acronym'][is_good], mapping='Beryl'))\n\n    times, units = np.concatenate(times), np.concatenate(units)\n\n    # sort the merged probes by spike time, as the reference does, so trials can be sliced\n    order = np.argsort(times, kind='stable')\n    return times[order], units[order], np.array(regions)\n\n\ndef spike_counts(times, units, n_units, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Spike count per unit and bin, for every trial.\"\"\"\n    counts = np.empty((stim_on.size, n_units, N_BINS))\n    begin, end = window_slice(times, stim_on)\n\n    for trial, onset in enumerate(stim_on):\n        spike_time = times[begin[trial]:end[trial]] - onset      # one flat index per spike lets a single bincount fill the whole unit by bin grid\n        flat_index = spike_unit * N_BINS + bin_index\n        counts[trial] = np.bincount(flat_index, minlength=n_units * N_BINS).reshape(n_units, N_BINS)\n\n    return counts\n\n\n# --- session ---\n\ndef process_session(eid, subject):\n    \"\"\"One session as lists of per trial neural, input and output arrays.\"\"\"\n    loader = SessionLoader(one=one, eid=eid)\n    loader.load_trials()\n    loader.load_wheel()\n    view = camera_view(eid)\n    loader.load_motion_energy(views=[view])\n\n    trials, wheel = loader.trials, loader.wheel\n    motion_energy = loader.motion_energy[f'{view}Camera']\n\n    variables = trial_variables(trials)                 # before filtering, so the block count is real\n    keep = usable_trials(trials, wheel.times.to_numpy(), motion_energy.times.to_numpy())\n    variables = variables[keep]\n    stim_on = trials.stimOn_times.to_numpy()[keep]\n\n    # no trial survived the mask, so there is nothing to load or bin\n    if stim_on.size == 0:\n        return {'session_id': str(eid), 'subject': subject, 'camera': view,\n                'neural': [], 'input': [], 'output': [],\n                'n_trials': 0, 'n_units': 0, 'regions': np.array([])}\n\n    speed = trial_traces(wheel.times, np.abs(wheel.velocity), stim_on)\n    whisker = trial_traces(motion_energy.times, motion_energy.iloc[:, 1], stim_on)\n\n    spike_times, spike_units, regions = load_neural(eid)\n    rate = spike_counts(spike_times, spike_units, regions.size, stim_on) / BIN\n\n    # the split is over the whole session, so both traces are discretized once, not per trial\n    speed_class = discretize(speed)\n    whisker_class = discretize(whisker)\n\n    neural = [trial.astype(np.float32) for trial in rate]\n    inputs, outputs = [], []\n    for trial in range(stim_on.size):\n        row = variables.iloc[trial]\n        inputs.append(np.stack([TIME, np.full(N_BINS, row.trial_in_block)]).astype(np.float32))\n        outputs.append(np.stack([np.full(N_BINS, row.choice), np.full(N_BINS, row.prior),\n                                 speed_class[trial], whisker_class[trial]]).astype(np.int8))\n\n    return {'session_id': str(eid), 'subject': subject, 'camera': view,\n            'neural': neural, 'input': inputs, 'output': outputs,\n            'n_trials': len(neural), 'n_units': regions.size, 'regions': regions}\n\n\ndef assemble(results):\n    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n\n    regions = sorted({region for r in results for region in r['regions']})\n    reg_ix = {region: i for i, region in enumerate(regions)}\n\n    return {\n        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n\n        'brain_regions': regions,\n        'brain_region_idx': [np.array([reg_ix[x] for x in r['regions']], np.int32) for r in results],\n\n        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description':\n                'Head-fixed mice turn a wheel to report the side of a visual grating. The '\n                'probability that the stimulus appears on the left is held constant within a '\n                'block and switches between 0.2 and 0.8.',\n            'time_bin_size': BIN * 1000.0,          # ms\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'visual stimulus onset (stimOn_times)',\n            'off_start': T_START,\n            'off_end': T_STOP,\n\n            'dataset': 'IBL brain-wide map, release tag %s' % RELEASE,\n            'neural_units': 'Hz',\n            'session_info': [\n                {'session_id': r['session_id'], 'subject': r['subject'], 'camera': r['camera'],\n                 'n_trials': r['n_trials'], 'n_units': r['n_units']}\n                for r in results\n            ],\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef worker_setup(datadir):\n    \"\"\"Give each process its own database connection, which cannot be shared across a fork.\n\n    datadir: directory holding the one_cache/ folder, forwarded from --datadir.\n    \"\"\"\n    connect(datadir)\n    one.load_cache(tag=RELEASE)\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    ap.add_argument('outfile', help='output pickle path')\n    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='process all sessions (default)')\n    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='process only 2 sessions')\n    ap.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=10, help='sessions to process in parallel')\n    ap.add_argument('--datadir', default=DATA_DIR,\n                    help='directory holding the one_cache/ folder (default: %(default)s)')\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    connect(args.datadir)\n    selected = select_sessions()\n    pairs = [(eid, subject) for subject, eids in selected.items() for eid in eids]\n    if args.sample:\n        pairs = pairs[:2]\n    print('%d sessions, %d subjects, %d workers'\n          % (len(pairs), len({s for _, s in pairs}), args.workers), flush=True)\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    # sessions are independent, so they are processed out of order and put back by index\n    ordered = [None] * len(pairs)\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers, initializer=worker_setup,\n                             initargs=(args.datadir,)) as pool:\n        futures = {pool.submit(process_session, eid, subject): index\n                   for index, (eid, subject) in enumerate(pairs)}\n\n        progress = tqdm(as_completed(futures), total=len(futures), unit='session')\n        for future in progress:\n            eid, subject = pairs[futures[future]]\n            try:\n                res = future.result()\n            except Exception as error:\n                print('failed %s: %s: %s' % (eid[:8], type(error).__name__, error), flush=True)\n                continue\n\n            # a session with no surviving unit or trial will be skipped\n            if res['n_units'] == 0 or res['n_trials'] < 2:\n                print('skipped %s: %d trials, %d units'\n                      % (res['session_id'][:8], res['n_trials'], res['n_units']), flush=True)\n                continue\n\n            ordered[futures[future]] = res\n            progress.set_postfix_str('%s %s %d trials %d units'\n                                     % (res['session_id'][:8], subject, res['n_trials'], res['n_units']))\n\n    results = [res for res in ordered if res is not None]\n    data = assemble(results)\n    print('\\nconverted in %.1f s' % (time.time() - t0), flush=True)\n    print('sessions %d | subjects %d | regions %d | trials %d | units %d'\n          % (len(results), len(data['subjects']), len(data['brain_regions']),\n             sum(r['n_trials'] for r in results), sum(r['n_units'] for r in results)), flush=True)\n\n    with open(args.outfile, 'wb') as f:\n        pickle.dump(data, f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)\n    print('wrote %s (%.1f GB)' % (args.outfile, os.path.getsize(args.outfile) / 1e9), flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","numLines":401,"startLine":1,"totalLines":401}}}

The AI applies several filters: (1) exclude trials with NaN in key event columns (stimOn_times, choice, feedback_times, probabilityLeft, firstMovement_times, feedbackType), (2) reaction time must be between 0.08s and 2.0s, (3) exclude no-choice trials (choice == 0), (4) max trial length filter (feedback_times - goCue_times <= 10s), and (5) a combined mask requiring valid wheel and whisker motion energy data for each trial's time window.\n\nii.\n```python\nNAN_EXCLUDE = ['stimOn_times', 'choice', 'feedback_times',\n               'probabilityLeft', 'firstMovement_times', 'feedbackType']\n\ndef create_trial_mask(trials):\n    mask = pd.Series(True, index=trials.index)\n    for event in NAN_EXCLUDE:\n        if event in trials.columns:\n            mask &= ~trials[event].isna()\n    rt = trials['firstMovement_times'] - trials['stimOn_times']\n    mask &= (rt >= MIN_RT) & (rt <= MAX_RT)\n    mask &= (trials['choice'] != 0)\n    if 'goCue_times' in trials.columns:\n        trial_len = trials['feedback_times'] - trials['goCue_times']\n        mask &= (trial_len <= MAX_TRIAL_LEN) | trial_len.isna()\n    return mask\n```\n\niii. The bin grid spans -0.5s to 1.5s (100 bins).\n\nii.\n```python\ndef bin_spikes_vectorized(spike_times, spike_clusters, n_clusters, interval_starts, interval_ends, binsize, n_bins):\n    binned = np.zeros((n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n    for trial_idx in range(n_trials):\n        bin_idx = np.minimum(((t_sel - t_start) / binsize).astype(np.int32), n_bins - 1)\n        flat_idx = c_sel[valid] * n_bins + bin_idx[valid]\n        np.add.at(binned[trial_idx].ravel(), flat_idx, 1)\n    return binned\n```\n```python\nneural_trials.append(final_spikes[t].astype(np.uint8))  # (n_clusters, n_bins)\n```\n\niii. Block boundaries are detected where `probabilityLeft` changes. The count is computed on ALL trials before filtering, so filtered-out trials still advance the counter. The AI reimplements the wheel processing: (1) interpolate position onto a 1000 Hz grid, (2) apply an 8th-order 20Hz Butterworth low-pass filter (sosfiltfilt), (3) differentiate to get velocity, (4) take absolute value for speed. The speed is then interpolated to trial-aligned bins using `interp1d` with `fill_value='extrapolate'`.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef interpolate_wheel(timestamps, position, fs=WHEEL_FS, corner_freq=WHEEL_CORNER_FREQ, order=WHEEL_FILTER_ORDER):\n    t = np.arange(timestamps[0], timestamps[-1], 1.0 / fs)\n    pos_interp = interp1d(timestamps, position, kind='linear')(t)\n    sos = signal.butter(N=order, Wn=corner_freq / fs * 2, btype='lowpass', output='sos')\n    vel = np.insert(np.diff(signal.sosfiltfilt(sos, pos_interp)), 0, 0) * fs\n    return t, np.abs(vel).astype(np.float32)\n```\n\niii. The binned data is stored as raw spike counts (uint8), NOT divided by the bin width to produce firing rates. Multi-probe sessions have their spikes merged with cluster ID offsets. The bin grid spans -0.5s to 1.5s (100 bins).\n\nii.\n```python\ndef bin_spikes_vectorized(spike_times, spike_clusters, n_clusters, interval_starts, interval_ends, binsize, n_bins):\n    binned = np.zeros((n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n    for trial_idx in range(n_trials):\n        bin_idx = np.minimum(((t_sel - t_start) / binsize).astype(np.int32), n_bins - 1)\n        flat_idx = c_sel[valid] * n_bins + bin_idx[valid]\n        np.add.at(binned[trial_idx].ravel(), flat_idx, 1)\n    return binned\n```\n```python\nneural_trials.append(final_spikes[t].astype(np.uint8))  # (n_clusters, n_bins)\n```\n\niii. The speed is then interpolated to trial-aligned bins using `interp1d` with `fill_value='extrapolate'`.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef interpolate_wheel(timestamps, position, fs=WHEEL_FS, corner_freq=WHEEL_CORNER_FREQ, order=WHEEL_FILTER_ORDER):\n    t = np.arange(timestamps[0], timestamps[-1], 1.0 / fs)\n    pos_interp = interp1d(timestamps, position, kind='linear')(t)\n    sos = signal.butter(N=order, Wn=corner_freq / fs * 2, btype='lowpass', output='sos')\n    vel = np.insert(np.diff(signal.sosfiltfilt(sos, pos_interp)), 0, 0) * fs\n    return t, np.abs(vel).astype(np.float32)\n```\n\niii.
